# Benson event-driven architecture implementation plan

**Canonical implementation Plan. Updated: 2026-11-09. Language: English.**

**Status: approved documentation target; implementation gated.** The initial DOC rewrite and Active-Run Progress correction passed validation/review and merged in PR #27. Oren subsequently approved the documentation-only Exclusive Domain Tool Ownership refinement; its scope and acceptance are in §§1.3 and 10.1. The earlier unified-entry refinement is recorded in §§1.4/10.2; the current approved Main/Domain documentation refinement and its acceptance boundary are in §§1.6/11.14. D5's proposed hours remain unapproved for runtime implementation. P01–P04 retain their bounded accepted evidence; P05 remains archived and unaccepted. The old P05–P11 prospective sequence is superseded by the ED stages below.

Authority: [Architecture](architecture/BENSON_SUBAGENT_ARCHITECTURE.md). Engineering procedure: [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md), [PLANS.md](PLANS.md). Physical predicates and device capability acceptance: [Jessica Full Capability Plan](agents/jessica-vacuum/FULL_CAPABILITY_PLAN.md).

**Current migration state, 2026-11-09:** TS05's approved Jessica status and Reminder full-list flows are **bounded Production WhatsApp E2E PASS**. Both follow authenticated WhatsApp → Request Controller → Main → domain Task → verified DomainResultV2 → Response Controller → native WhatsApp delivery, with exactly two effective Main business tools, no Domain Run or business mutation, and one human request/reply per flow. OpenClaw2026.10.10 uses the approved two-owner workspace correction and pinned WhatsApp Response integration. Gateway is intentionally disabled/inactive/dead, PIDs0, cron=false, Restart=always restored; no activation follows from publication. Failure/recovery evidence and the verified after-checkpoint remain intact. Native completion receipts and the checkpoint CLI defect are explicitly limited in §11.64; neither reopens the accepted reads. Mandatory canonical cleanup, final integrated review, publication, Oren-owned merge and post-merge snapshot remain workflow gates. Broader Phase1 and ED01–ED07 obligations remain incomplete; §1.5 step2 is next, subject to G1(b)/G2 and its own exposure approvals.

## 1. Objective, scope and approval boundary

Replace the General Workflow design with authenticated input admission, independent native Run lifecycle and controlled Domain Completion, durable domain operation ownership and native event/schedule/delivery mechanisms. Remove prospective JOIN/CLOSE, membership revisions, shared finals, retained orphaned lifecycle state and a global dependency/capacity engine. Preserve Main's bounded semantic coordination, strict completion validation, evidence-based recovery and proven domain safety.

The target is a Deterministic-First, Domain-Owned, Event-Driven Agentic System under Architecture §§1/4/8.1/9. Generic admission routes direct, Main-delegated, event and Automation tasks to one logical owning-domain entry; adequate structured intent requires deterministic handling, and only necessary reasoning requests a fresh authorized domain Run. Main remains the central cognitive orchestrator and sole user-facing agent without a mandatory model hop. Request disposition, Run outcome, physical outcome and delivery outcome remain distinct. A finished Run is never resumed to await a device. Durable Event-Triggered Conditional Actions remain explicitly deferred and visible as a release limitation.

### 1.1 Approved D1–D6 decisions

| Decision | Target | Implementation limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D1 | Shared HA ingress under existing `benson-routing`, using native plugin service lifecycle; domains own event predicates and device mapping. | New service registration/binding needs its implementation-stage approval and proof. Nothing registered here. |
| D2 | Independent input disposition; native Main lifecycle without mandatory Completion v5 in Phase 1; one controlled Domain Completion family for agentic domains. | Domain terminal/correlation proof and common Response enforcement remain required; no engine or second completion store. |
| D3 | Active-only steering may be added after exact native provenance/generation/race proof. | Deferred, disabled; no silent follow-up, interrupt or new-Run fallback. |
| D4 | Source-scoped background authority, domain notification eligibility and native durable outbound. | No fabricated human requester; event-to-Run and delivery handoff remain explicit proof gates. |
| D5 | Explicit domain-local pending-cleaning FIFO in existing Jessica state, stable original identity and domain-appropriate start window. | Proposed practical default in Jessica §7.4 requires explicit policy approval before implementation. No 30-minute pending expiry or timeout-based release. |
| D6 | Deferred single source predicate → single preauthorized target action, e.g. successful exact cleaning → create Reminder. | Retain capability and minimum contract; resolve native record/claim/binding before implementation. No general dependency engine, new persistence infrastructure or scheduler. |

### 1.5 Generic Foundation v1 — approved delivery priority, 2026-10-10

Phase 1 delivers small working vertical slices through the existing ED owners: generic trusted admission/routing; one deterministic execution-mode selector per domain; shared validated no-Run/Completion consumption and Response/native delivery; Jessica and Reminder integration; representative multi-domain user-facing E2E and actual latency/model-call/token/reliability evidence. Adding a domain must require its own capability/authorization/validation/wording integration, not new generic orchestration branches. Main remains the cognitive and sole user-facing agent. Deterministic structured work adds no classifier/domain model call or child Run; reasoning-required grants nothing and launches nothing.

Deliver in this order: (1) ED02/ED03 Main-delegated Jessica status and Reminder full-list reads with shared no-Run response preparation; (2) the bounded ED02/ED03 genuine-reasoning path only after its G1(b)/G2 binding; (3) a bounded ED04 known-event path and relevant ED06/ED07 integration/E2E, reusing accepted native Reminder scheduling instead of introducing Jessica scheduling. Consumer migration and production acceptance follow each exact enabled batch rather than waiting for unrelated ED04/ED05 capabilities. The approved implementation below covers only step 1's source, tests, Main instructions, registration metadata and this Plan; no runtime publication is included.

Phase 2 defers exhaustive authorization-negative matrices, extreme cancellation/revocation races, speculative bypass exploration and advanced hardening unnecessary for the bounded enabled capability. Essential authenticated admission, currentness before read/disclosure, domain permission/tool ownership, strict result validation and fail-closed behavior remain prerequisites. Mutations retain existing safety/idempotency/current-authority guarantees. A demonstrated unsafe exposure is not deferrable: disable that capability and continue independent safe work. Current Main `full` policy and missing native G1/G11 evidence remain recorded release limitations; reprioritization does not close them or accept whole ED01/ED02.

Keep every dated gate and acceptance obligation below. Foundation acceptance names its exact enabled origins/capabilities and proves the applicable A03/A07/A13/A17/A20/A22 subset at runtime/user-channel level; actual Run, event, notification, physical or scheduled additions also require their applicable gates. It is not whole-stage PASS or acceptance of disabled matrix rows. G9/G10 apply only if their classifier/fast-path capabilities are proposed. Steering, Progress, pending cleaning, conditional actions, subscriptions and broader physical/scheduled Jessica functionality remain outside this bounded slice and need their existing separate approvals/proofs. This priority change removes no architecture invariant or security control.

### 1.5.1 Limited Phase 1 runtime acceptance — operator decision, 2026-10-11

Oren approved progressing the working Foundation while deferring exhaustive G11 indirect-bypass isolation and advanced Agent Guardrails research to Phase 2. For this subset only, the exhaustive indirect-bypass isolation proof within the G11 prerequisite in the prospective stage/acceptance rows and earlier dated blockers is deferred under Architecture §8.1's bounded exception; exclusive domain ownership remains the target, not a proven property. G11 stays OPEN, ED01 stays formally BLOCKED, and no whole ED stage is accepted. Main retains its full general capabilities, Code Mode and existing native domain-tool restrictions. No sandbox, structural redesign, core modification or speculative hardening is introduced. This decision approves the Plan correction and preparation; runtime exposure and each canary retain their exact approval gates.

**Enabled acceptance scope:** authenticated Main-delegated Jessica `status` and Reminder full `list` reads only, using their existing native task entries and the common admission/no-Run/Response owners. Prefer direct schema-defined calls; domain selection/execution/verification is deterministic, with zero additional classifier/domain model calls or child Runs. No new physical operation, Reminder mutation, reasoning Run, event/Automation ingress or independent notification/durable-outbound path is included. Existing integrations and mutation safeguards are preserved, not reaccepted or expanded.

**Minimum safety and proof:** preserve host-issued requester authority, the existing domain permission/visibility checks, live invocation currentness across awaits and before reads/disclosure, strict correlated result/evidence validation and fail-closed handling. Do not invent authority or soften missing-context/unknown-version/invalid-result rejection. Reuse accepted source/currentness and native direct-denial evidence where bytes and boundary remain applicable; establish loaded parity for the selected entries and domain-only raw tools. Actual user-channel acceptance must prove authentic Main ingress and each native task invocation/result. Essential G1 checks for this read subset are not waived; its broader negative/race matrix stays unproven. A concrete unauthorized disclosure, raw-tool exposure or failing guard blocks that specific path and requires repair before continuation. Full general-tool indirect-bypass resistance is explicitly unproven, not inferred from prompt compliance or a successful canary.

**ED06 → ED07 sequence:** verify the reviewed source and a restorable runtime checkpoint; obtain exact exposure approval; reload only the two existing domain plugins through the supported native lifecycle, with no permissions/provider/config change; verify applied generation/source identity, registrations and unchanged policies. Then obtain action-time approval for one representative real Main request per domain. Correlate requester visibility, complete model-visible facts/Response-prepared content, one task execution and one native reply, with no child Run/fallback. Evaluate final answers against user intent: natural rephrasing, grouping and variable detail are expected; factual inaccuracies or materially misleading omissions block acceptance, not non-verbatim wording or omitted immaterial fields. Record observed model calls, timestamps/latency, tokens/cost and reliability; missing telemetry remains UNKNOWN. A canary using the legacy child path or bypassing Main cannot accept this slice. Preserve the 17:17 BLOCKED and 21:31 limited old-path PASS results; neither proves the new two-domain path.

### 1.6 Main/Domain reliability refinement — approved documentation direction, 2026-10-22

Phase 1 targets native Main lifecycle without mandatory Main Completion v5; deterministic Domain Entry → execution/verification → DomainResultV2 without Run identity; and agentic Domain Entry → authorized native Run → enforced tool evidence → controlled Domain Completion → DomainResultV2. Both domain modes and Main composition use one deterministic Response Controller. Architecture §§4.3/4.5/6/8.2 own the boundaries; §11.14 maps the work into the existing TS migration. Design approval, source readiness, native integration proof and production acceptance are separate. No unproven agentic path or universal Main interception is activated by this refinement.

Only optional native Main Completion evaluation is newly deferred to Phase 2, indexed in PHASE2_BACKLOG.md. Existing advanced deferrals and the exact §1.5.1 read exception remain; essential authorization, tool isolation, result/effect preservation, Domain Run correlation/settlement and common Response requirements are not deferred. Preserve Completion v5 implementation/tests and all historical acceptance. This task changes only Architecture, this Plan and the backlog; runtime instructions/domain plans remain unchanged. Implementation of material contract, custody, storage or lifecycle deltas needs its exact operator decision before code changes, with no new stage, reordered TS01–TS05 or second implementation track.

### 2.2 Read-only event/native feasibility, 2026-10-09

HA `2026.5.4`; Dreame manifest `v2.0.0b23`, embedded library `v2.0.0b24`; device model `r2469a`, firmware `4.3.9_1642`. Snapshot `output/context/benson-context-20261009-103609.md` was captured at 10:36:11 IDT and retains its own freshness limits.

Eight authenticated WebSocket subscriptions were acknowledged. A 42-second observation captured an attribute-only `docked → docked` event at 07:43:22 UTC; no live completion occurred. Recorder evidence for October 2–8 contained task 24, error 4, warning 7, consumable 40 and information 1 events. No retained sample for an event is not non-support.

Actual vacuum entity `vacuum.jesica_jesica` and event alias `vacuum.jesica` require canonical registry binding; `did` is a device identifier. Task events do not represent every pause/resume. Stop/dock can optimistically mark COMPLETED and trigger listeners before the command POST. Public history loses distinctions among interrupted/manually ended/failed outcomes; its timestamp is a start time, and an exact active-task bridge/requested-room result is unproven. A completed event contradicted history. Fresh HTTP fetching does not prove independently refreshed device data.

Installed OpenClaw inspection established public `registerService` lifecycle, `runtime.subagent.run`/wait/messages primitives and durable outbound functions at source level. It did not establish event-to-Run tool authority, universal terminal enforcement, stable native message handoff or physical completion. Current upstream/official research and alternatives are recorded in Architecture §27.2; no version upgrade or loaded-byte equivalence is implied.

Preserve Jessica's Full Capability Plan and all dated FC evidence. Accepted scope includes no-settings single-room cleaning and current-task pause for all 13 reviewed rooms/five approved family members; unordered exact `living_room` + `hallway` clean/pause for the same family; guest-bathroom-only dock and strong-suction setting. FC0 is PASS; FC1 and remaining FC2 are BLOCKED. Other multi-room sets, full-home, ordering/repeats, broader settings, same-task resume, stop and exact completion are not accepted by this rewrite.

Preserve Reminder's deterministic atomic plans, creator/recipient policy, canonical conversation provenance, timezone/relative schedule calculation, native Automation lifecycle, Calendar linkage, idempotency, rollback/compensation and verified readback. Event-driven architecture does not justify relaxing its current Main-bound trust boundary. That binding is trusted conversation provenance, not permission for Main to invoke Reminder tools; Reminder owns Reminder operations only.

## 4. Versioning, migration and native ownership

### 4.1 Common completion successor

Completion v5 is the existing source-validated successor to private v4; preserve its schema, code, tests and evidence. Its Phase 1 target use is controlled Domain Completion, not mandatory Main settlement. Preserve legacy meanings and strict readers while identified consumers/durable records exist. Current request wrapper v1, route/result v1 and response/render v2 consumers require migration; v3/v4 constructors/tests are not evidence of production-wide adoption.

Completion v5 removes `workflowId`, membership/accepted-intent revisions, first-member final anchor and closed-membership requirements; remaining consumer migration must preserve that separation. Do not reinterpret them as Run IDs or drop legitimate vendor program IDs. Separate completion eligibility, parent continuation eligibility and outbound eligibility. Replace the old equation of finality with `RESPONSE_CONTROLLER` targeting.

Preserve the tested completion budgets and their source/fixture evidence in §4.4. Test lossless recovery at boundaries and native carrier acceptance; changing a budget is an explicit contract decision. Unsupported version/malformed input follows fail-closed settlement preserving effects, never a semantic fallback.

Inventory all producers, consumers, durable records and test fixtures before migration. Use an explicit version compatibility matrix and rollback path; do not dual-write to a second result store. Drain or retain old native records and frozen deliveries until safely interpretable. Remove a legacy reader only after reference checks plus supported replay/retention evidence.

ED02 must resolve the following matrix against actual loaded consumers; ED06 verifies it before exposure. A row is a migration obligation, not permission to invent a binding or silently upgrade a record.

| Surface / existing owner | Producer → consumer migration | Compatibility / rollback obligation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| User, HA and Automation admission; `R/request-control` plus domain identity | Source entrypoints and Main delegation → common admission with trusted authority → the same owning-domain entry | Retain P03 custody and merged ED02 v1 contracts; reject unknown source/version/grant. Explicit v2 same-invocation read admission uses native currentness and domain read permission under Architecture §4.1; call identity is not original input or mutation authority. Prove the actual Main carrier under G1/G11 before enabling it; no synthetic requester, duplicate registry or mandatory child Run. Install compatible readers before enabling new producers. |
| Domain Run completion; `R/core/envelope`, domain completion and `R/core/completion-control` | Controlled domain consumer plus captured tool evidence/native binding → Completion v5 validation → DomainResultV2 → Main or Response | Explicit legacy-version readers for inventoried records only; preserve effects, original outcome and bounds. Unknown/malformed completion rejects or remains unresolved with effects preserved; FAILED v5 also requires valid binding. Never invent success, binding or replay. |
| No-Run result; existing admission/domain result contract and `R/response-control` | Domain validator → eligible native Main result return or Response gate; reasoning-required → same admission/native activation boundary | Preserve merged ED02 v1 variants under Architecture §4.5; prove Main return/correlation without a fake child completion, duplicate reply or parallel result owner. A reasoning disposition grants no authority; G1(b)/G11 and other applicable gates precede a Run. Unknown versions cannot authorize output/effects; retain uncertainty for reconciliation. |
| Jessica operation/episode/pending state; existing `jessica-operations-v1` owner | Fenced executor/verifier → current domain readers and dispatch guards | Preserve v2 accepted records and original identity; atomically migrate only reviewed shapes. No conversion from started to completed; older writers cannot act on newer records. Disable affected writes if rollback cannot interpret them. |
| Scheduled domain definition/occurrence; owning domain plus native Automations | Authorized domain registration → native job → same domain authorization/executor/verifier | Inventory existing jobs and readers; version semantic payload/authority binding, retain original requester/grant, native job/occurrence/revision and effect history. Reject unknown or unverifiable legacy bindings; no automatic adoption as operator authority. Compatible readback/reconciliation precedes enablement; rollback disables affected new jobs without replay or erasing uncertain effects. No parallel job store or new schema owner. |
| Wording and delivery; domain templates, Response and native outbound | DomainResultV2 or native Main composition without Main Completion → one outbound gate → native intent/receipt | No dual-send during cutover. Reconcile domain claim with existing native intent; old frozen payloads/routes/history survive rollback without regeneration. |

Cut over one explicitly approved source/capability batch after its readers, grants, validators and reconciliation pass. Keep new routes disabled until those prerequisites exist; drain or quarantine incompatible in-flight work through its current owner. Rollback disables new admission/dispatch first, then restores only compatible consumers while retaining every known/possible effect and native delivery reference. Never blindly down-convert durable state, enable old unsafe writers or replay an uncertain action. D6 record migration is deferred to ED-C1 after G7/G8; this matrix creates no conditional-action record now.

### 4.2 Domain state and fencing

Extend existing Jessica namespace/device schema only in its approved stage, with explicit version, atomicity, old-record handling, migration and rollback. Original input/operation identity must survive fresh Runs. Preserve requester/grant reference, intent and map revision, resource generation, step ownership, known/possible effects and history. Retired or timed-out owners must fail immediately before POST. Neither Run settlement nor pending expiry releases the device.

No new completion, admission, event, conditional-action or delivery database is approved. If supported native/existing owners cannot hold a required invariant, record the exact gap and stop the dependent design for approval; do not quietly add an abstraction.

### 4.3 Native binding decisions and alternatives

Architecture §27.2 contains the current primary sources and decision rationale. Apply them to exact installed bindings:

- Prefer shared `registerService` lifecycle over Jessica-specific watchers. Prove startup/stop/reconnect/backpressure and source filtering; no model event polling.
- Prefer deterministic domain reconciliation; use `runtime.subagent.run` only where fresh reasoning is necessary and trusted tool authority is proven. `deliver:false`, an idempotency option or a plugin owner marker does not supply a human principal. `runContext` is transient. Current-requester dispatch closures cannot be captured and reused after their authorized callback.
- Reject official-install-only `gateway.request` / `dispatchHookAgentTurn` as a local authorization workaround. Inspected provenance values include `external_user`, `inter_session`, `internal_system`; do not invent `system_event` support.
- Native `enqueueSystemEvent` is an in-memory, coalescing existing-session mechanism, not a durable fresh event-to-Run authorization contract. `waitForRun` timeout does not cancel work.
- Prefer native domain terminal observation plus controlled deterministic result consumption (§11.14); Main uses native lifecycle without mandatory v5. Accepted P01 source evidence at PR #18 commit `681ea91e59c8bee753093dee9c77208799f1c4fd`, this Plan's historical §6.4 row G01/O08, records the 2026-10-03 audit of installed `docs/plugins/hooks/prompt-and-session.md:271–301` and `dist/hooks-CKanWLsK.mjs:525–584`: `before_agent_finalize` can request revision of natural finals, excludes user abort, has a 15-second budget/default fail-open policy, and can be overridden by force-finalize; `agent_end` is observational. That same record says Copilot lacked the hook and Codex relayed native Stop. These are dated source observations, verified here against retained P01 evidence, not newly exercised timeout/harness behavior or a current universal-coverage claim. P01 explicitly left the all-terminal/all-harness binding and its fixtures unproven. Reverify the actual enabled host/harness before implementation; none of these Agent Run hooks defines deterministic no-Run handler acceptance.
- Prefer `sendDurableMessageBatch` with required durability and supported durable intent context. Prove stable business identity adoption and native receipt reconciliation, including the domain/native handoff crash gap. Internal delivery APIs/options are not public-plugin permission. Provider acceptance is not user reading.
- For D6 prefer native Automations condition watcher/job lifecycle. `trigger.script` / `once` state and native run receipts are useful, but `once` follows successful payload execution, force-run may bypass the trigger and the inspected service Cron facade does not prove direct HA event wakeup/atomic target claim. The target boundary must independently validate predicate, validity, authority and effect identity. Standing intents/orders do not supply that transaction. No custom scheduler is the fallback.

**Exclusive ownership research, 2026-10-09:** inspected installed OpenClaw `2026.9.6` documentation at `docs/automation/cron-jobs/payloads.md` and `docs/tools/multi-agent-sandbox-tools.md`, and current primary sources linked in Architecture §27.2. Installed docs describe headless code-mode script payloads using owner tools, but describe command payloads as an operator-admin surface outside model-visible exec policy. Current upstream tool-policy semantics differ in details; neither document proves the deployed binding. Jessica read/execute factories and the Reminder plugin already require their own `agentId`; current Jessica execution also requires a fresh child session, and Reminder retains Main conversation provenance. These are source observations, not proof of headless authority or comprehensive isolation. Prefer supported native execution into those existing domain boundaries and reuse `shared/benson-datetime.mjs`; a scheduled LLM adds an unnecessary dependency for fixed work, and extending Reminder to control Jessica violates ownership. Generic exec/command access is not an authorization substitute. No payload kind, new callback/adapter, registration or permission change is selected here. G11/G12 require exact native proof; otherwise keep the affected capability blocked.

### 4.4 Preserved implementation budgets

Legacy values below remain unchanged. The explicitly approved DomainResult v3 / Response v4 successor budgets are separate contracts in the existing owners (§11.43); they do not enlarge legacy readers or defaults. Existing implementations enforce each version and preserve lossless facts through consumer adaptation.

| Contract / evidence owner | Preserved numerical contract | Evidence limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P03; `R/request-control.mjs` and its context tests | Up to five exact relevant prior messages; retrieval tiers 256 KiB / 256 events, 512 KiB / 512 events and 1 MiB / 1,024 events; at most three retrieval attempts and at most one Decision call. | Retained P03 source/native-fixture acceptance in §2.1. The budgets do not guarantee that sufficient or authorized context exists. |
| P02; `R/envelope.mjs` and contract/response tests | Up to 16 results; usable wording at most 4,096 UTF-16 code units; 64-item warning/effect/gap collection bounds; legacy 256 KiB / depth-16 JSON bounds. | Retained P02 contract acceptance in §2.1, not a native transport limit. Preserve lossless facts/reconstruction and actual per-variant validation. |
| P02 private v4 aggregate; same completion owner | Each component retains 256 KiB / depth-16. Aggregate byte bound is `262144 * (resultCount + 1) + max(0, resultCount - 1)`; with 16 results, the derived bound is 4,456,463 bytes and embedded aggregate depth reaches 18. | Historical validated source/fixture capacity, not proof of installed native-carrier acceptance. A successor version must explicitly preserve or review changed bounds. |
| Approved DomainResult v3 / Response v4; `R/core/value.ts`, `domain-result.ts`, `legacy-response.ts` | B=262144 serialized UTF-8 bytes. DomainResult v3 inherited payload <=B, metadata <=B, envelope <=2B=524288. Response v4 metadata <=B; up to16 individually validated v2 (<=B) / v3 (<=2B) results, including known-version mixtures. Aggregate <=B+sum(result version caps)+max(0,n-1); absolute maximum8650767 bytes. Existing per-component depth16, field/count and rendered4096 UTF-16 limits remain unchanged. | Approved source compatibility invariant; representative isolated native composition still requires observed acceptance. It does not assert maximum native transcript/model/carrier capacity or authorize lossy projection. |
| D5 domain policy; Jessica Full Capability Plan §7.4 | Target capacity remains two pending cleaning requests per device, excluding active work; exact proposed start-window values and separate policy-approval gate remain in that domain owner. | Proposed domain policy, not an implemented/validated pending queue. No inherited elapsed 30-minute expiration. |

**Pi sizing evidence and production gate (ED07).** On 2026-10-05, Raspberry Pi 5 Model B Rev 1.1, 8 GiB RAM, Node v24.21.0/arm64 and installed OpenClaw 2026.9.6/eb377ac: five fresh synthetic native profiles, three full-read samples each, measured 129–2049 events / 210933–2416629 bytes. Full `openAsync` took 21–157 ms with roughly 1.3–13 MiB additional heap. The near-limit profile (1024 events, 900252 bytes) also measured three bounded reads: 42–69 ms, 6.4–6.9 MiB additional heap and at most 6 MiB positive RSS delta, using native maxEvents 1022. Its stats reads took 0.95–4.19 ms. At 2049 events / 1410061 bytes, full reads reached 157 ms, 12.8 MiB additional heap and 26 MiB positive RSS delta. This supports conservative independent 1 MiB/1024 ceilings below the larger profiles, preserving headroom rather than deriving a maximum from available RAM. Peak process RSS (including SDK imports, fixture production and native workers) was about 530–578 MiB; GC/worker reclamation can produce negative deltas, so these samples do not prove a process-memory cap. Evidence: `output/p03-native-fixture-measure-{XD9jZv,2a5FCt,dOjmoD,5m9nF3,VzZYUd}/hydration-measurement.json`, produced by the existing native test's `--measure-hydration` mode with `--expose-gc`; zero model calls. Tier 1 exceeds the measured small profile (129 events / 210933 bytes, full-read 21–27 ms and 1.3–1.6 MiB additional heap); tier 3 keeps the measured conservative 1 MiB/1024 ceiling. Tier 2 is the fixed doubling step between these measured anchors. Geometric growth caps aggregate read work at 1.75 times the absolute payload budget and avoids an unexplained new maximum; it is a source policy, not a measured latency percentile or production concurrency guarantee. Before production activation, ED07 must verify these source-owned ceilings under representative Pi concurrency, cold/index-maintenance and retention conditions, recording latency and memory impact; a threshold change requires justified code/plan review. Oversized targets remain Main-only without full hydration. Source/native fixtures do not establish production readiness. The supported seam follows [OpenClaw session transcript hydration](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/agent#session-transcript-hydration), verified against the installed public declarations and implementation.

The Architecture retains boundedness, evidence preservation, privacy and compatibility invariants. This relocation grants no larger context/payload, new persistence surface, or permission to discard information when a native carrier cannot meet the existing contract.

## 5. Explicit implementation proof gates

A gate is OPEN until the required evidence exists at its stated level. Source presence, option names and synthetic native fixtures cannot close higher levels. A failed gate blocks only dependent work; retaining a disabled/deferred capability is not acceptance of it.

| Gate | Missing guarantee / responsible owner | Minimum evidence and dependent stages |
| --- | --- | --- |
| G1 Trusted input and event-to-Run authority | Routing/native runtime plus affected domain identity owners. Independently prove (a) each source's admission/read/reconcile/notify grant and (b) fresh Run/tool authority where a Run is needed; preserve original delegated mutation grants without forging a human. | ED01 establishes native feasibility/supported binding evidence and unresolved guarantees; ED02 implements/tests missing source-grant admission and typed no-Run contracts under its bounded entry gate; ED04 proves the integrated HA event path. Isolated source/identity, revoked/foreign/stale grant and restart tests, then runtime and approved production identity proof remain required. Enabled deterministic paths need (a); new direct/background tool Runs additionally need (b) with actual native tool bindings. Passing (a) never closes event-to-Run proof. |
| G2 Controlled Domain Run settlement | Existing native lifecycle, domain evidence and Completion owners. Controlled Completion is mandatory; full native-generation export in v5 is not the only acceptable binding. | For each enabled path prove actual launch/caller/Run/tool correlation, live authority, domain fencing, complete coverage of approved calls, zero-tool handling, verified facts/effects and truthful terminal or unresolved disposition. Foreign/stale/duplicate/missing/unfinished evidence cannot become success. Unproven yield/resume/harness paths stay disabled. Preserve restart/effect reconciliation without duplicating native lifecycle. Exhaustive lifecycle coverage is deferred under §11.27; any incompatible bounded representation requires an explicit reviewed version in the existing Completion family. Isolated native proof precedes separately approved channel/physical acceptance. |
| G3 Response and native outbound | Existing Response Controller and native delivery owners; approved bounded Phase 1 under §§11.27/11.30, without SDK/core changes. | Prove original enabled flows through mandatory Controller preparation and existing native delivery: success, handled tool failure, supported error/timeout/fallback candidates; actual request/Run/domain custody and trusted route at their boundaries. Preserve native lifecycle/writer metadata. Controller rejection/cancellation must prevent an uncontrolled fallback or duplicate handoff. Truthful failure/UNKNOWN and no uncertain replay remain mandatory. Independent universal post-handoff enforcement, uncovered senders and recovery are Phase 2 hardening; an actual bypass in an enabled flow still blocks acceptance. |
| G4 Active steering | Native active-run mechanism plus admission owner. Proven input consumption/application and terminal/generation/cancel race. | Exact supported active-only behavior; rejected terminal input cannot create, queue or interrupt another Run silently. ED-S1 only; disabled while deferred. |
| G5 Jessica exact completion and release | Jessica FC1/core/state/HA verifier. Independent identity/generation, requested scope, successful terminal predicate, release/readiness and retired-dispatch reconciliation. | Installed public observation path, contradiction/replacement/restart fixtures and separately approved representative physical proof. Event boolean/current cache alone fails. ED04 completion subset, ED05 advancement and ED-C1 source predicate. Trusted device error/warning alerts need FC7 evidence, not exact task correlation; they cannot close G5. |
| G6 Pending default start policy and wakeup | Jessica domain policy and operation state; native wakeup binding. | Oren's approval of Jessica §7.4 exact proposed hours/late behavior before implementation; atomically stored absolute bounds, expiry/cancel/claim/restart/fairness tests and supported native wakeup. ED05. |
| G7 Conditional record, claim and source-event binding | Reminder target owner, native Automations; Jessica predicate owner. Concrete one-record host, lifecycle authority and atomic claim/cancel; supported wakeup/reconcile. | One authoritative record mapping, native permission/API proof, deadline and force-run guard, source binding, recovery, schema/retention/migration contract. No new store by inference. ED-C1 deferred. |
| G8 Conditional target authorization/idempotency | Reminder target transaction and existing native state. Original delegated authority valid at registration/effect; cross-Run transaction lookup after lost acknowledgment. | Revocation/cancel/claim/late event tests; duplicate events and crash-after-create-before-ack resolve to one verified Reminder transaction. Explicit content/time/recipient/late semantics and accepted source proof. ED-C1 deferred. |
| G9 Real provider evaluation and routing quality | Existing `R/{classification,policy,rubric,evaluation,telemetry}`, corpus and native provider owners. Preserve baseline P10/P11's real Decision evaluation, approved thresholds and non-dispatching shadow before a direct cohort. | ED06: prove the exact native provider/model/mode, bounded data/egress, constrained output, zero tools where required, deadline/abort and budgets; evaluate an independent held-out corpus across language/context/domain/risk/privacy cases. Record false-direct selections, unavailable/error rates, calls/tokens/cost/latency and fallback fidelity; approve numeric sample/error/latency/cost/cohort criteria before shadow. ED07: bounded observational shadow with the Main-admitted path as sole execution commitment and domain-owned execution, then separately approved read-only and mutation cohorts only after measured criteria pass. Synthetic classifier outputs/no-provider probes cannot close this gate. |
| G10 P03 production read readiness | Existing P03 context/custody read owner and installed native transcript path. Preserve the baseline §6.6/P11 production gate independently of source payload ceilings. | Before enabling the P03 fast path in ED07, measure representative Pi concurrency, cold reads, index maintenance and retention conditions, recording latency and memory impact against reviewed operational limits. Preserve source ceilings; any threshold change requires justified code/Plan review. Historical synthetic measurements in §4.4 do not prove production percentiles, memory caps or concurrency readiness. |
| G11 Effective domain tool isolation | Existing native tool policy and domain adapter guards; Architecture §8.1. Main and non-owning agents may delegate but cannot access another domain's tools or bypass them. | ED01: inspect effective loaded capabilities and prove positive owner/negative Main/foreign-agent cases per proposed harness/path, including reads, writes, forged agent IDs, generic exec/code-mode/raw HA/native-job bypass and delegated-context changes. Prompt rules and factory name checks alone are insufficient. ED02/ED06 and domain stages require the relevant proof before new exposure; runtime parity remains required. |

Prove execution-time revocation, cancellation/edit/disable/force-run fencing, duplicates, restart and lost-ACK reconciliation through the real native path. A documented payload kind or fabricated tool context is insufficient. FC9 T01 and any other scheduled domain operation require their exact binding; unproven means BLOCKED, not a privileged wrapper or mandatory LLM fallback. |

G1, G3, G5, G11 and G12 are explicit release blockers for their relevant capabilities and may not be described as already proven. G2 is similarly mandatory for new agentic domain Run paths; Main response safety remains under G3, not a synthetic Completion. Native service availability cannot close any of them by itself. G12 does not block unrelated interactive/event evidence, and headless no-Run processing does not require G2 unless an actual Agent Run is created.

**Main-to-domain entry/result binding — G1/G11, OPEN:** identify the exact supported installed native path by which an authenticated Main task reaches the existing owning-domain entry and returns a validated outcome without exposing internal domain tools to Main or creating a domain LLM Run. ED01 must record version/harness/API, host-issued requester custody, live invocation authority, domain permission and same-call result correlation. Through that real path, prove a sufficient structured read task uses zero additional Decision/domain model calls and zero child Runs; reject foreign/forged/missing/revoked provenance, cancellation before read/disclosure, unauthorized destination/tools and generic-exec/native-job bypass. Prove eligible Main return and no second reply. A repeated pure read may re-admit with current authority; a lost/restarted invocation cannot retain its handle. Original input/operation identity, duplicate/restart/uncertain-effect reconciliation and current mutation authority remain mandatory for state-changing paths, not prerequisites for a pure read. Separately, a genuine reasoning-required case must pass G1(b)'s actual fresh Run/tool binding and G2; the disposition alone cannot launch it. No synthetic authority, core patch/fork, privileged wrapper or mandatory LLM workaround is accepted. Existing event/scheduled proofs cannot substitute for this distinct delegated binding. Keep dependent Main-entry routes disabled until accepted; preserve PR #30 v1 contracts through explicit compatible readers.

G9/G10 retain previously required activation evidence; they introduce no provider client, routing owner or runtime exposure. If real Decision evidence/approved criteria are unavailable, record PROVIDER-BLOCKED and keep Decision routing off; only independently proven Main safety may be accepted as a limited scope. The optional Response Model has a separate real-provider constrained-invocation/fallback evaluation before it is enabled; missing provider evidence requires an explicit deterministic-fallback disposition, not fabricated acceptance. No household-data egress, provider setup, inference, shadow or cohort activation follows from this documentation approval. Deterministic no-Run event alerts do not depend on Decision evaluation or the P03 context fast path unless their actual implementation uses those capabilities.

## 6. Jessica pending cleaning and deferred conditional actions

### 6.1 D5 implementation contract

Architecture §18.4 owns system semantics; Jessica Full Capability Plan §7.4 owns the concrete default-policy proposal. Preserve that division rather than copying policy values into routing defaults.

ED05 implements only opt-in domain-local FIFO with at most two pending entries in the existing Jessica operation owner. Admission persists original operation/principal, target/map/intent/policy revisions, ordering and source/predecessor identity, absolute start bounds and cancel/claim/effect state. Request response may say accepted as pending only after durable readback.

The head is eligible only after its exact predecessor succeeded and the resource is independently ready, with all grants/bindings/window checks still valid. Failure, cancellation, replacement and uncertainty of active work block automatic advancement; require an authorized disposition. Removing an expired/canceled pending entry cannot masquerade as predecessor success or automatically retarget a later dependency.

Do not ask ordinary opt-in requests to supply a deadline when an approved practical default applies. The current proposal is concrete and justified in the domain plan, but is not yet approved for implementation. It bounds when a pending request may start, permits ordinary long cleaning/recharge within the same operation, never stops active cleaning and never slides to tomorrow after restart. Explicit next-day or different-window intent follows native scheduling/domain policy, not a hidden queue extension.

Native wakeup is a liveness aid; atomic domain checks enforce safety even after lost/delayed wakeups. Same-request duplicates preserve bounds/order. Cancellation before claim prevents dispatch; after possible dispatch it requires reconciliation. A failed/uncertain cancellation does not release the robot.

### 6.2 D6 retained capability and unresolved owners

Architecture §17.5 is the minimum normative contract. ED-C1 is deliberately deferred, with no implementation/persistence/registration in this documentation stage. It remains an architectural capability and an explicit release limitation until accepted.

Resolved logical ownership: trusted admission owns initial authority; Jessica owns the exact successful source predicate; Reminder owns target creation semantics and transaction reconciliation; native Automations is preferred for durable registration/lifecycle/wakeup; native outbound owns optional delivery. Main understands the request but is not the durable owner.

Unresolved concrete bindings that must be decided before implementation:

1. Which existing native record is authoritative, how target-domain ownership is enforced and how atomic revision/claim/cancel are exposed to the local plugin.
2. How HA verification wakes the record through supported native interfaces; scheduled reconciliation must have a reviewed latency/late policy and cannot be marketed as immediate event delivery.
3. How original principal/grant and target action digest bind into a fresh deterministic invocation or native Run without impersonating a current human turn.
4. How the existing Reminder transaction accepts stable conditional-effect identity and reconciles an already-created job/Calendar transaction after lost acknowledgment.
5. How source operation/generation and completion time are proved, and how expiry, time anchors, retention, semantic edit and cancellation races map to existing native/domain state.

If native hosting is insufficient, compare a minimal extension of existing target-domain native persistence with leaving the capability deferred; request a separate owner/persistence decision. Neither capability approval nor this Plan permits a general dependency mechanism or new infrastructure. Registration must remain unavailable until durable readback and all required bindings work.

## 7. Ordered implementation stages

Stage-specific scope, dependencies and acceptance gates below remain binding. Root AGENTS.md governs approval inheritance, recoverable baselines, review and publication. Required production/channel/physical exposure must be named in a current action-time approval envelope under that owner; stage approval alone does not authorize it. Common rollback is affected compatible source/config restoration, disabled new exposure and reconciliation of known/possible effects; never overwrite all native state, erase histories or replay mutations.

Domain FC1 supplies physical correlation/release evidence; it does not wait for ED07 to investigate its own predicate. No dependency requires General Workflow infrastructure. Any release that intentionally excludes an unmet base-stage requirement needs an explicit scope/acceptance decision; do not silently relabel the stage PASS.

FC9 is not a prerequisite for independent ED04 alerts, nor does G12 reclassify existing accepted Reminder evidence as scheduled Jessica proof.

The unified-entry refinement adds no stage or implementation approval. ED04 retains event-grant/predicate dependencies; ED05's resolved queue mechanics remain deterministic under its existing G5/G6 prerequisites. G1(b) gates fresh domain Run authority; G2 applies to actual agentic domain Runs; Main uses native lifecycle without mandatory Completion v5, and deterministic results require no child Run. G12 gates scheduled execution and G3 outbound delivery. ED06 verifies origin/mode compatibility and ED07 accepts each enabled class at its required level. The merged ED02 source-only slice remains intact; these obligations neither restart it nor close any gate.

### ED01 — Native boundary proofs and binding decisions

**Objective/owners:** isolate G1–G3 and G11/G12 in existing routing/domain/native test owners; identify supported APIs and exact installed version. Preserve P05 negative evidence. Produce the concrete binding/permission/lifecycle mapping before implementation commitment. Fresh source/upstream research is required if assumptions changed since §2.

**Work:** minimum native probes for each source's supported entry and authority custody where the existing contracts permit proof, §5's Main-to-domain no-Run entry/result binding, fresh tool Run where needed, all enabled domain Run terminal causes, mandatory Main Response interception and frozen durable handoff. Record G1 source-grant versus Run-authority evidence separately, and G2 versus G3 separately, so deterministic work is not blocked by unrelated Run hooks. Reuse merged ED02 admission/no-Run contracts rather than fixture-local substitutes; remaining missing contracts belong to ED02. Full HA event integration/E2E belongs to ED04. Compare supported native boundaries with keeping dependent features gated. Native primitive proof needs separately approved exact isolated exposure; no production mutation, core patch/fork or persistent orchestration owner is authorized by this refinement.

**Acceptance:** exact supported contract and isolated evidence for each proposed binding; negative authority/race/restart cases; state every unproven runtime step. A required unresolved binding blocks its dependent implementation, except the narrowly bounded ED02 contract work defined below, which implements the missing boundary without enabling its dependent path. That entry exception establishes neither full ED01 nor G1(a) PASS and waives no native/runtime/security proof. G4/G7/G8 may remain explicitly deferred without blocking unrelated evidence work.

For G11/G12 record installed version/harness, effective tool inventory and native invocation provenance, original grant, job/occurrence/revision, zero-model evidence, observed effects and rejected calls. Test initial semantic interpretation separately from deterministic fire-time execution. Main/Reminder attempts to register, alter or execute Jessica jobs outside Jessica must fail; an authorized Jessica path must succeed in the isolated fixture. Pause/cancel/revoke/edit before the final dispatch claim yields no effect; a prior possible effect remains recorded for reconciliation. Exercise native retries, force-run, restart and crash-after-effect-before-ack without a second dispatch. No test may mint a Main requester, call core directly around authorization or use a second adapter to manufacture PASS. Source-only evidence keeps the corresponding native gate OPEN.

**Checkpoint/rollback/exposure:** source checkpoint and only any separately approved isolated fixture state. No production config, provider/channel canary or device dispatch is included by default. Accepted source/isolated level is explicitly insufficient for ED07.

#### ED02 bounded source admission/no-Run contracts — 2026-10-10

**Approval/scope:** Oren approved implementation, focused validation and independent review under the entry gate. EXTEND only `R/request-control.mjs` and `R/envelope.mjs`; add one focused contract test in the existing test owner. KEEP all ingress/domain/native owners and accepted P01–P04 implementations. No producer is registered or connected; no domain executor, grant issuer, adapter, store, dispatch, Run, job or delivery is introduced. ED01 remains BLOCKED and its native guarantees remain OPEN.

**Private contracts:** source input v1 (`kind: benson.source-input`) carries a closed source type/ref/inputId, target domain/scope and exact request, bounded by the retained 8192-byte request budget. `createSourceAdmissionController` requires explicit synchronous source authority, currentness, domain-outcome and clock dependencies. Its source owner must bind the exact input outside payload data. The current authority record binds input/source/domain, subject, authority reference/revision and permitted read/reconcile/notify/action scopes; active, unexpired, uncancelled, fresh and uncommitted state is required. Missing/untrusted/foreign/stale/revoked/unreconstructable or uncertain records reject before domain handling. The immutable in-process admission handle cannot be recreated by JSON or another controller. This branding is correlation only, not durable replay protection or an admission commitment.

The separate `benson.no-run` v1 variant in the existing envelope owner carries admission correlation, one of handled/no-change/suppressed/rejected/reasoning-required/unresolved/failed, predicate evidence/freshness, none/known/possible effect references, required reconciliation, optional notification candidate and typed failure. It retains the existing 262144-byte JSON carrier, depth/string/collection bounds, sixteen effect references and 4096-character rendered content limit. Possible effects require reconciliation; malformed or unknown versions cannot reach domain acceptance. A notification includes business identity, eligibility/policy revision, evidence reference, recipient/route and content; it requires the domain's permission/evidence/eligibility assertion plus a current source notify scope. No-change does not itself authorize or deduplicate a notification. Authority is rechecked after domain validation and on later use; reasoning-required preserves effects and installs no alternate route.

**Producer/consumer compatibility:** future authenticated user/HA/Automation entry → Request Controller → existing domain assertion → no-Run contract; actual native custody/issuer and domain callback wiring remain unresolved and unimplemented. Jessica `lib/results.mjs::validateResult` and Reminder `dist/contracts.js::normalizeReminderServiceResult` remain their evidence owners, with existing interactive authorization unchanged. Fixtures use the implemented generic source/result boundaries and the existing Jessica evidence validator; supplied dependency records/assertions are contract inputs, never host-issued grants or notification acceptance. `CONTROL_CONTRACT_VERSION=1`, request wrapper v1, result/route v1, Response v2 and completion v3/v4 readers remain intact. No-Run does not pass through Run completion or current Response consumers; their integration remains ED03. There is no state migration or durable new format to reconstruct; restart loses the handle and requires supported re-admission, which is not proven here.

**Decision/evidence:** choose a disabled extension of existing contracts rather than fixture-local validation or live registration. Current [OpenClaw public service documentation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/gateway-and-nodes) remains feasibility evidence only; [OWASP authorization guidance](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html) supports deny-by-default and per-use checks, not invented source authority. No ED01 probe was repeated. Focused tests cover all source/disposition variants, foreign/forged/missing/expired/revoked/replay/uncertain state, cross-controller/restart-lost handles, bounds, domain-evidence rejection, notification scope and post-await/post-domain revocation. Exception/timeout/restart cases prove typed preservation of supplied possible effects, not live handler reconstruction or crash recovery. Affected legacy envelope/Response compatibility tests are run because their shared module changed; unchanged native suites are not rerun.

**Acceptance/gates:** acceptance is source/unit contract evidence only; native integration, production/channel and physical E2E are deferred because this slice has no exposure. Source test output, exact input/diff hashes, checkpoint and fresh independent review verdict are retained in `output/ed02-source-contracts-20261010/`; mandatory review must pass before slice closure. G1(a)/G1(b), G2/G3, G11/G12, atomic native commitment/replay/restart authority, original grant custody, domain source authorization and scheduled execution remain unresolved. #### ED02 completion v5 source-only contracts — 2026-10-10

**Approval/scope:** Oren approved this separate SIGNIFICANT slice after accepted PR #31/main sync. EXTEND only `R/envelope.mjs`, add `R/tests/completion-v5.test.mjs`, and record slice acceptance here. KEEP Architecture, merged ED02 source/no-Run v1, strict legacy readers/defaults, domain executors and all native/runtime owners. No issuer, producer connection, handler, schema owner, store, settlement hook, dispatch, delivery or migration is installed. ED01 remains BLOCKED; this approval is not a general exception to its gates.

**Private successor:** explicit completion v5 stays in the existing completion family and uses its authority constructor, agent proposal validator, evidence equality and deterministic reconstruction. `domain-task` reports one actual owning-domain Run; `agent-run` reports an actual Main/other Run with zero to sixteen independently validated domain Run results. A direct domain Run needs no synthetic final wrapper; a Main answer needs no domain child. No-Run dispositions never enter this family. v5 rejects the legacy `final-workflow` kind, `workflow`/`semantics` evidence, membership/accepted-intent revisions, first-member anchor, `role` and `finality`; it removes `workflowId` from lifecycle bindings but preserves legitimate vendor program IDs in domain facts.

Bindings retain explicit input/admission/authorization references, Run/session identities and opaque generations, domain/task identity and an optional exact native caller tuple. An actual headless Run has null conversation ID/generation together, never an invented conversation. Domain facts must match the existing domain/agent mapping; FAILED reconstruction can retain explicitly missing facts as gaps. Aggregate children must retain the exact parent Run/session tuple and conversation binding; distinct input/admission references survive without workflow membership, and duplicate task/Run results reject. Authoritative facts, effects and original operation IDs are copied losslessly; proposals cannot supply bindings or rewrite facts. `NORMAL`/`RECOVERED`/`FAILED` remain completion quality, independently of business status.

`completionTarget` is the private logical disposition `CALLER`, `RESPONSE_CONTROLLER` or `NATIVE` (settlement without either handoff). Explicit `parentContinuation` and existing `deliveryPolicy` eligibility/reason records are independent of settlement: denied continuation/outbound still permits a valid report. Eligible continuation requires a bound caller and CALLER target; eligible outbound requires RESPONSE_CONTROLLER targeting. These are trusted-owner snapshot projections, not grants, live authorization checks or installed API names. Currentness, host custody, cancellation, actual handoff and outbound eligibility must still be enforced by their supported native/domain/Response owners before exposure. No model, callback, I/O, replay or execution is added to reconstruction.

**Compatibility/producer-consumer inventory:** repository source reference inspection found the private constructor/readers in `R/envelope.mjs` and their contract/Response tests; it does not inventory live native records or establish loaded runtime support. Existing `R/completion-control`, `R/response-control`, domain completions, core patches and durable owners remain unchanged. Architecture §27.2 and §4.3's current installed/primary-source evidence are reused; no native binding alternative is newly selected or probed. Choose explicit v5 over silently reinterpreting v4 or retaining a synthetic General Workflow wrapper.

### ED03 — Settlement, response ownership and native delivery

**Objective/owners:** existing completion/response owners, domain result/templates and native lifecycle/outbound. Eliminate retained engine settlement/shared-final paths while preserving all effects.

**Work:** the Domain Run slice enforces controlled evidence-based Completion for every enabled agentic domain Run, with valid native binding for all completion qualities and effect-preserving unresolved rejection when binding is missing. Optional same-Run repair cannot execute/delegate. The shared outbound slice accepts native Main composition without Main Completion, validated DomainResultV2 from either domain mode, eligible nonterminal Active-Run Progress under the contract below, or validated no-Run domain outcomes at the same Response gate; the domain alone owns notification eligibility, business key/revision and claim. Completion settlement grants no new notification entitlement. Migrate valid central templates once into domain owners. The explicit-unavailable Response Model fallback applies only to valid Run completion; Progress never invokes it and no-Run output is deterministic. Freeze authorized payload/route and bind native intent before transport.

**Slice prerequisites:** outbound/no-Run acceptance needs the ED02 no-Run contract, G1 source grant and G3; it does not require G2 or fresh event-to-Run tool proof. Run acceptance additionally needs the corresponding ED02 Run contract, G1 Run authority and G2. Keep whole ED03 incomplete until both slices meet their obligations; record any accepted subset explicitly.

**Delegated no-Run outcomes:** integrate only after §5's G1/G11 Main-entry/result proof and relevant ED02 contracts. Validate/correlate the same domain outcome for an eligible active Main without fabricating a child Run/completion or adding a model repair step. Test deterministic success, malformed/unresolved outcome, possible effects, cancellation/terminal requester and duplicate result return; preserve effects and prevent a second reply. Main settles through native lifecycle without mandatory v5; a no-Run domain result has no child-Run completion obligations. Outbound eligibility remains under the single Response/G3 boundary.

**Active-Run Progress — Architecture §6.5:** an optional, typed nonterminal proposal from an actual active Run, validated at the existing Run/domain result boundary and admitted only through the existing Response Controller to native OpenClaw outbound. The current Main/domain execution owner supplies bounded evidence-based content. It is not a Run completion or a deterministic no-Run notification; no synthetic event/Run, `benson_complete`, Response Model or alternate send path is used. Progress is best effort: denial, suppression, missing/failed/unknown delivery must not replay execution, create or resurrect a Run, establish physical success, release resources, replace mandatory Domain Completion or block native Main/domain terminal settlement. Later domain events use their independently authorized no-Run notification contract, never retired Progress authority.

- **Trusted eligibility:** derive caller, input/operation reference where applicable, Run ID/generation, destination/account/conversation/thread and current grant from supported native authority, not model arguments or the session's latest route. Select allowed Progress types and evidence predicates from the existing trusted execution/domain policy. Missing/disabled policy, unknown binding, revoked authority, stale/replaced/canceled/terminal Run or completion-only repair denies Progress. The Response Controller enforces that policy without becoming another domain eligibility owner.
- **Bounds and identity:** require reviewed finite per-Run logical-message budget, rate/minimum interval and payload/provider-unit bounds; no numeric defaults are approved here. Atomically reserve eligibility, a trusted native invocation/event identity and budget before native handoff, using supported existing native Run/lifecycle metadata and existing domain evidence. Failed or dropped admitted attempts remain charged; duplicates retain their identity/result without another charge or send. Equal text or a model-supplied ID is not deduplication authority. Concurrent calls and restart cannot reset limits. Existing domain business claims still prevent repackaging the same alert as Progress. No new store, ledger or workflow-wide counter is introduced; absent a supported atomic binding, keep the path disabled.
- **Terminal/cancellation race:** prove native serialization of Progress eligibility with generation retirement/cancellation/terminal entry, including delivery-time checks and suppression of queued unsent work and retries after retirement. An active check only at enqueue is insufficient. Completion takes priority and never waits for Progress delivery or reconciliation. If transport has already made an effect irreversible, retain its native receipt/uncertainty, do not blindly resend or manufacture cancellation, and let terminal settlement proceed. Queue acceptance/provider acknowledgment is not proof of physical outcome or user reading. Do not claim active-only delivery from an admission check or timing guess.
- **Native proof gate:** ED01/G1–G3 must establish the supported active-loop invocation, authoritative policy/budget/identity state and active-only outbound behavior for each enabled harness/channel before Progress activation. Exact binding and any necessary API/registration remain unproven and require their existing implementation approval; no surface is registered here. If those delivery guarantees cannot be established, explicitly record Progress as BLOCKED/DISABLED. This blocks Progress, not independent Completion or no-Run evidence work; a rollout omitting it must state the limitation under the existing stage acceptance rules.

**Progress acceptance within ED03:** extend existing response/lifecycle/domain/native fixtures; record exact host/harness/channel versions, trusted invocation shape, Run/generation and policy revision, native custody/receipt references, assertions and evidence level. Do not store raw household transcripts. Source or simulated callback tests alone cannot prove an active native loop or production delivery.

| Test case | Required observed result / evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Active loop and eventual settlement | One real isolated authorized native Run emits multiple distinct allowed Progress proposals and continues executing. Progress causes zero completion submissions, no ownership/resource transition or stronger physical claim. Native Main lifecycle or controlled Domain Completion, according to the actual path, settles independently. |
| Authorization and factual boundaries | Foreign caller, recipient/account/thread override, stale generation, revoked grant, missing policy, repair-only phase, malformed/oversized content and unsupported physical claim are rejected before native send. A no-Run notification cannot borrow active-Run authority, or vice versa. |
| Concurrent limits and duplicates | At budget/rate boundaries, concurrent proposals, duplicate callbacks and restart retain the same authoritative counters/identity; limits reject deterministically without ending the Run. Chunking cannot escape payload/provider-unit bounds; duplicate Progress/domain-alert proposals do not create a second business send. |
| Retirement and cancellation races | Hold a proposal before claim, between claim and native custody, and queued before send/retry; race cancellation, terminal entry and generation replacement. A retirement winner permits no new Progress send. Native suppression/ordering is evidenced, not inferred; already irreversible/unknown effects retain custody without delaying Completion or blind resend. |
| Delivery failure and recovery | Missing adapter, timeout, failed/unknown acknowledgment and crash after native handoff leave valid execution and native Main lifecycle / mandatory Domain Completion intact. Recover the same native intent/receipt without replay, a new reporting Run, resource release or a parallel queue. |
| Runtime/channel promotion | After isolated acceptance, separately approved ED07 evidence proves the enabled active-loop binding, continued execution, independent native Main/domain settlement and actual intended channel behavior. Keep any unproven Progress path disabled; never substitute deterministic no-Run alert acceptance for this proof. |

**Validation/acceptance:** every enabled domain Run terminal cause/harness, malformed completion after a real/simulated committed effect, denied tools during repair, duplicate completion, terminal parent and valid/explicit-unavailable/malformed wording. Separately test typed no-Run acceptance/rejection, no model fallback, and a single domain notification claim across HA, Automation and reasoning-Run candidates; stale/conflicting payloads require reconciliation, not another send. Exercise native handoff crash phases and failed channel independent of successful execution. Use supported native bindings; hash-matched internals alone do not establish acceptance. Runtime/channel evidence remains due at ED07, or at an explicitly approved earlier bounded canary.

**Checkpoint/rollback:** affected code and native lifecycle/delivery metadata if approved. Preserve frozen pending deliveries and effect histories across rollback; no regenerate/resend. Fail closed when supported lifecycle or native intent recovery is absent.

### ED04 — Shared ingress, verification and proactive alerts

**Objective/owners:** extend `benson-routing` with native service lifecycle; Jessica remains predicate/state owner. No device-specific shared transport in its tool plugin. Domain alert policy comes from FC7; physical predicates from FC1.

**Work:** ingress owns source-authenticated allowlisted WS transport, bounded parsing and reconnect/backpressure/gap recovery. Generic Request Controller validates routing authority and canonical source/domain bindings; the owning domain entry owns device mapping, event semantics, action selection, bounded reads and reconciliation. Known structured events require zero LLM calls and validated no-Run outcomes; domain notification eligibility includes clear/re-arm, severity transitions and stable device/condition-episode/kind/recipient keys across all input paths. For cleaning initiated through an app, button or device schedule, trusted device/error evidence can authorize a warning without inventing a Benson operation or requester. It cannot prove task success/failure, release physical ownership or advance pending/conditional actions. Only domain-declared necessary reasoning may request a fresh background Run, with G1(b)/G11 and G2 satisfied. Scheduled reconciliation uses reviewed native Automations with explicit latency, not an LLM loop/new scheduler.

Notification subscriptions and generic operation-notification recipient selection belong to ED04, using the existing ED02 admission/no-Run contracts and ED03 Response/native outbound path.

**Validation/acceptance:** alias mismatch, unauthorized source, malformed/huge input, attribute-only change, duplicate/reorder/missing event, disconnect/restart, stale cache, contradictory terminal/history, replacement and missed completion; alert threshold/severity/suppression/clear/re-arm, cross-path conflicting candidates and Oren route identity. Prove an external-start device warning with unknown task correlation and unchanged physical ownership/pending state; missing trusted device/error evidence must suppress it. Prove exact G5 physically before enabling completion claims or resource release. Warning/maintenance alerts can be accepted independently of G2/G5 with FC7 predicates and the deterministic admission/outbound proofs. FC9 records domain delivery acceptance using this ED04 slice; whole FC9 PASS is not an ED04 prerequisite. Verify requester-default and subscription-based recipient selection, natural-language subscription management and persistence across restart.

Prove known-event zero-model handling, no domain semantics in ingress/general routing, canonical mapping rejection instead of entity-name guessing, and authenticated-but-unclassified events with evidence-only Decision use when necessary. Unknown bindings/grants remain denied even if a model proposes a plausible domain/action; no mode change may replay effects or bypass source/domain authority.

**Checkpoint/rollback/exposure:** source plus exact approved plugin registration/config/native job batch checkpoint; supported config validation and minimal reloads. Live read-only subscription does not authorize device commands or channel sends. Disable newly exposed ingress/notifications before rollback, preserve operation/episode and native delivery evidence, reconcile gaps. Source-only PASS is not production acceptance.

### ED05 — Pending cleaning

**Objective/owners:** existing Jessica operation store/core, domain policy and native wakeup. Implement Architecture §18.4 and Jessica §7.4 only after exact default-policy approval; no global Run queue.

**Work:** versioned domain state extension with atomic bounded FIFO admission/claim/cancel, stable original identity, persisted absolute start window, eligibility/readiness fences, expiry and truthful pending result. Resolved pending intent enters the same Jessica boundary; claim, wakeup, expiry and advancement are deterministic with zero LLM calls, regardless of original direct/Main admission. Never extend windows by restart or independently auto-next after failed/canceled/replaced/unknown active work. Preserve approved physical capability scope at dispatch.

**Validation/acceptance:** long cleaning over 30 minutes and same-cycle recharge; successful exact predecessor/release; third pending request capacity; concurrent family admission; duplicate input; changed map/grant; head blocked/expired/canceled; no overtaking/retargeting; cancel-vs-claim; crash around POST; start-window edges/DST/local-date rollover; delayed native wakeup; no next-day dispatch after expiry. One separately approved representative physical pending-to-start canary plus deterministic equivalent cases; do not infer physical G5 from fixtures.

**Checkpoint/rollback:** versioned existing state and source/config/native wakeup baseline. Disable pending admission/advancement, preserve every pending/claimed/uncertain record and settle or explicitly carry it forward. Do not erase pending commitments or stop active cleaning through a file rollback.

### ED06 — Migration and cutover readiness

**Objective/owners:** actual consumers, runtime Main/domain instructions and native registrations. Replace obsolete engine assumptions without losing accepted capabilities or changing unrelated code.

**Work:** §4.1 compatibility table complete for actual versions/loaded consumers; map each loaded old/core dependency to supported replacement; converge direct/Main/event/Automation inputs on existing domain entries and migrate their completion/no-Run/template consumers through common admission/Response ownership. Inventory native Main task/result carriers, trusted bindings and origin/mode grants; preserve merged ED02 contracts and accepted provenance. Remove mandatory child-LLM assumptions only with proven compatible native entry/result custody, never by granting Main domain tools. Align runtime contracts/tools only after underlying proof; drain old records/frozen deliveries safely. Prove mixed-version rejection and compatible rollback before enabling each source/capability batch. Remove dead exports/patch dependencies only after inspected references and accepted parity. Archive historical evidence rather than reactivating obsolete stages.

For domain-tool/scheduled migration, retain existing accepted provenance until a compatible replacement is proven; do not widen Main/Reminder access to preserve a shortcut.
