### 11.52 TS04 measured resource acceptance gate — 2026-11-02

**Verified checkpoint.** Fresh configured independent read-only review PASS accepts the 26-path §§11.50–11.51 candidate frozen in review-3-candidate.json, with review-3-validation.json/review-3.md in the existing pending-v2 checkpoint. This is the pending-binding/Reminder native product slice, not full TS04. The following measured finding and unapproved proposal are outside that frozen review; publication requires fresh integrated review after any material continuation.

**Failure and owner.** A smallest isolated native regression now demonstrates the previously open §11.47 resource gap. Actual supported readers on installed 2026.10.4 acquired a small synthetic original in 199 ms. A later non-user append caused the retained original-source guard to decode one 1,049,074-byte native event before rejecting against the unchanged 1,048,576-byte ceiling. The guard fails closed for domain execution, but rejection after hydration violates the preserved P03 resource invariant: candidate IMPLEMENTATION DRIFT at the read boundary. There were zero domain/production effects. The test used a disposable network-isolated bwrap namespace, read-only SDK/output and temporary state/home, with JSON.parse observation only; installed Core was not modified. The evidence log, method and installed-source hashes are retained in resource-gate-evidence.json/resource-growth-probe.log. Exact accepted Plan bytes are retained before this gate note.

**Supported alternatives assessed.** The canonical conversation adapter uses readSessionContext for synchronous generation/prefix revalidation. Installed session-accessor.sqlite-model-context scans all navigation before the callback; session-DitLEF2L hydrates each yielded payload before the adapter can count its bytes. Strict Task snapshots compare rawSeq before payload iteration, but original/retained custody deliberately permits own non-user appends. Current [official hydration documentation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/agent#session-transcript-hydration) recommends bounded asynchronous views; installed openBounded[Async] measures bytes before loading, but its public prepared view does not expose the generation/rawSeq custody needed to replace this synchronous guard. A separate size/stats preflight leaves a snapshot race and itself scans aggregates; async raw/visible pages do not supply a synchronous pre-effect check. readSessionContext accepts admission only, without limits or a through fence; openModelContext's through requires a native-issued anchor, not a fabricated stats token. The metadata-only version validator exported through codex-session-transcript-runtime is explicitly private-local, not for third-party plugins. Pre-write hooks are not committed-history evidence. These alternatives do not establish the complete bounded, current, same-snapshot invariant under the present public adapter contract; this is not a claim that every possible upstream/native solution has been disproved.

**Operator disposition — no Core/SDK exception.** Oren explicitly prohibited additional Core/SDK patches and authorized adapter-only correction or disabling the affected history-dependent path, while continuing independent safe TS04 assembly. The earlier optional bounded synchronous-reader proposal is not approved and is not a prerequisite. No cached authority mirror, private Codex import, new state/lifecycle owner, larger budget or race-based preflight is introduced. The installed public APIs assessed above do not currently prove the required bounded synchronous custody; the existing Benson conversation adapter therefore rejects native original-history acquisition before SDK import or transcript reads. Both v1 and v2 agentic Reminder requests require that acquisition before child launch and remain disabled; unavailable history is never empty history. Isolated explicit-reader contract fixtures remain source evidence only. Earlier native continuation PASS is retained as dated evidence of its bounded synthetic scenario, not acceptance of the now-disabled default path. Re-enable only after proving pre-hydration byte/event bounds and same-snapshot currentness through supported native APIs within the existing owner; a broader redesign requires approval. Existing TaskEnvelope v1 shape and validation remain unchanged. Full TS04/TS05 acceptance, dependencies and preserved capabilities are unchanged, with no Phase2 deferral or reduced release scope. Production, push and merge remain excluded.

Credential and HTTP access remain lazy in the canonical HA adapter and are replaced only by its existing injected ports in isolated tests.

The default-history regression observes zero SDK session-reader resolutions and zero payload access before rejection. Actual separately loaded Main/Reminder/Jessica/Response isolated E2E PASS: four scenarios (Reminder list, blocked v1, blocked v2 with explicit null selection, Jessica status), eight local provider requests, one list transaction, two synthetic HA GETs, two domain-result deliveries and no child Run or mutation. The namespace had zero external interfaces/routes and no host-home visibility. Before the passing run, the first run proved v1 rejection and one list transaction but failed a fixture assumption that originalIsError must be true for a thrown tool exception; the installed carrier actually records originalIsError:false and isError:true. After checking side effects, the fixture was corrected to assert the observed hook error, invalid domain-success carrier and absent effects, then rerun in a fresh namespace. No SDK correction was made. Artifact inventory covers three entries, 70 non-test/source-resource/dependency files (933,737 bytes); copied canonical resources match exactly. Original/continuation checkpoint bytes and modes, protected index/DREAMS, four installed Core owners and 12 deployed frozen files remain unchanged. Native product fixtures retain only synthetic provider/socket/HA/backend effects and the previously approved isolated patch pins; no additional Core/SDK bytes are changed. Full TS04 remains INCOMPLETE: history-dependent continuation, assembled Jessica agentic execution/Completion, complete effects/presentation coverage, concurrent admission/interruption/recovery and target resource headroom remain open. The disabled-path limitation is explicit and must not be promoted to full assembled release acceptance. Recovery uses before.json/before/ for the original slice and resource-safe-before.json/resource-safe-before/ for this continuation, including absence of the new Jessica entry/test; retain the last accepted review-3 candidate and unresolved resource evidence. Do not restore the unsafe default history reader as a candidate repair. Source rollback never undoes external effects; deployed dist and runtime are unchanged.

Select the canonical new Jessica/Reminder entries, without importing legacy execution or Completion owners for compatibility. No new tool/registration/lifecycle/state/transport owner, Core/SDK patch, mutation flow or unrelated development is authorized. New unsupported intents, agentic Runs, raw tools and history-dependent paths remain unavailable and fail closed, without fallback or replay. Do not run doctor --fix, alter durable ownership, delete orphan state or blame corrupt data without supported diagnosis and separate necessary approval.

### 11.64 TS05 bounded Production acceptance and publication closure — 2026-11-09

**Accepted scope.** Jessica status at09:31 and Reminder full-list at10:04–10:05 Israel time passed real WhatsApp E2E. Each original human input traversed the canonical Request Controller before Main, the owning Task's deterministic read, a verified DomainResultV2, Response authorization and matching native WhatsApp delivery. Oren confirmed one relevant reply per single request, without extras. Jessica was explicitly accepted from linked evidence without repeating the read after a punctuation-only acceptance mismatch. Reminder read the actual native store: two jobs, zero canonical managed Reminder jobs, matching the creator/recipient-authorized empty result. Populated/cross-user negative behavior remains isolated evidence, not additional Production acceptance.

**Owners and safety.** KEEP the existing Request/Domain/Response owners and native lifecycle/transport. Main's effective business tools are exactly jessica_task and reminder_task; native tool_search/dispatch_action are carrier controls, not internal domain permissions. Both domains used no Domain Run, no model repair and no business mutation (effects=none). Source refusal, missing authority or unverified DomainResult still fail closed. The approved Maintenance Verifier/global native-state comparisons and their dedicated blockers were removed from the active E2E path; retained historical files/checkpoints are evidence, not a reusable active verifier. Checkpoint/private restore, input/session correlation, effective permissions, separate action-time canary approvals, domain verification, Response/delivery and owned shutdown remain required. No new runner, validator or framework is introduced by closure.

**Workspace correction / approved Core exception.** Production correlated evidence showed workspace-mismatch rejecting borrowing from the full Gateway registry: config-wide inventory carried one workspace's identity into a prepared Main execution with another admitted workspace. Cold tool-discovery then lacked the full owner's Response binding. The approved structural correction clears a single workspace identity from config-wide metadata while retaining registryIndex provenance; prepared lending/reuse uses the native-normalized admitted execution workspace and rejects conflicting explicit metadata. The [exact two-owner diff](integrations/openclaw/patches/openclaw-2026.10.10-workspace-ownership.patch) is published as a retained exception, not an auto-apply mechanism. Qualified isolated R2 evidence covers19 native branch cases,11 metadata-consumer bypass cases and both canonical read flows; the later Production reads establish working binding at the actual enabled boundary. No global authority fallback, forced full registration or tool permission expansion is used. The earlier human failure's exclusive causality remains partial; this closure does not invent missing historical events.
